CHAIRMAN’S STATEMENT
           STATEMENT
主席報告




The year 2018 saw sustained improvement in the internal                  2018 年 ， 中 國 造 紙 行 業 內 部 環 境 持 續 優
environment of China’s paper manufacturing industry. Driven by the       化。在供給側改革驅動下，造紙行業去產
supply-side reform, the paper making industry made significant           能效果明顯，供需格局進一步改善。同
progress in eliminating obsolete production capacity, which resulted     時，政府對各項環保及節能降耗指標要求
in a more balanced supply and demand situation. In the meantime,         不斷提高，執法力度不斷加強，污染較高
with the increasingly stringent requirement on all environmental         的造紙企業逐步關停，行業集中度得到提
protection and energy conservation and emissions reduction               升，龍頭企業獲取了更多的市場份額，議
indicators as well as the tougher enforcement actions, paper             價能力也更強。整體形勢，有利於理文造
manufacturing enterprises which discharged a large amount of             紙。
pollutants were shut down one-by-one, leading to a more
concentrated market, with leading enterprises occupying greater
market shares and gaining stronger bargaining power. The overall
environment proved beneficial to the development of Lee & Man
Paper.


In the latter half of 2018, the slackened demand for downstream end      2018 年 下 半 年 ， 下 游 終 端 紙 製 品 需 求 呈
paper products dragged down the profit margin of the entire              現疲軟態勢，造成行業利潤降低。本集團
industry. To ensure the supply of raw materials, the Group has been      一直致力於通過積極整合上游資源，發展
committed to developing a vertical business model covering pulp          涵蓋製漿及廢紙回收的垂直業務模式，確
manufacture and recycling of waste paper through active                  保了原材料的供應；同時，本集團不斷擴
consolidation of its upstream resources. In the meantime, by             充規模，控制成本，提升產能，使得本集
expanding scale, controlling costs and strengthening production          團的主營業務依舊平穩向好的發展。提升
capacity, the Group's principal business achieved a healthy and stable   發展規模，穩抓機遇依舊是理文造紙未來
growth. Expanding scale of development and seizing opportunities         的主要目標。本集團積極優化產業鏈，拓
remain the key objectives of Lee & Man Paper for the coming years.       展紙漿多元化業務，同時，在原有的生產
The Group will make a proactive effort to optimise the industrial        規模的基礎上，積極響應「一帶一路」的步
chain and diversify the pulp business, while at the same time giving     伐，瞄準東南亞，拓展國際產業布局。
active support to the “Belt & Road” strategic initiative and targeting
Southeast Asia for international development riding on the existing
production scale.

主 席 報 告（續）




Lee & Man Paper has endeavoured to improve the quality of               理文造紙一直在努力改善提高生活用紙的
consumer tissue and boost its scale of production. To accommodate       質量和生產規模，為了迎合市場與客戶的
the needs of markets and customers, the new tissue production lines     需要，理文造紙江西廠房的新衛生紙生產
at the Jiangxi plant of Lee & Man Paper will commence operation this    線 將 於 今 年 投 入 生 產 ， 2019 年 衛 生 紙 的
year. As such, the production volume of tissue in 2019 can be           生產量得以提升，衛生紙業務已成為本集
enhanced. The tissue business has become the new growth driver of       團新的增長動力。
the Group.


The packaging paper business of the Group is also maintaining           本集團的包裝紙業務也在平穩發展，包裝
steady development, with the annual production volume reaching          紙總年產達618萬噸；而由於中國的廢紙
6.18 million tons in aggregate. Still, changes in China’s waste paper   政策及全球廢紙市場的變化，本集團的廢
policy and the global waste paper market have had some bearing on       紙回收受到了一定影響。因此，本集團積
the waste paper recycling of the Group. Therefore, the Group will       極尋求其他方法替代廢紙入口。
actively seek alternative methods for the import of waste paper.


From the general economic development pattern, there is further         從整體的經濟形勢發展來看，中國市場的
room for growth in paper consumption of the Chinese market and          用紙量仍具向上增長空間，本集團對造
the Group remains confident in the future of the paper manufacturing    紙業未來抱有相當信心。理文造紙將抓住
industry. Moving forward, Lee & Man Paper will seize opportunities      機遇，進一步拓展市場，提升產品的競爭
to expand its market coverage, reinforce the competitiveness of its     力，加強本集團在行業的領先地位。
products and cement the Group’s leading position in the industry.


In the future, while enhancing overall economies of scale, the Group    未來，本集團將在提升企業經濟效益的同
will stay focused on developing paper manufacturing techniques that     時，努力研發更加環保節能的造紙技術，
are more environmentally-friendly and energy efficient, with a view     以求在企業發展與良好生態環境之間尋求
to identifying a win-win path of sustainable development as it aims     雙贏的可持續發展的道路。
to strike a balance between corporate growth and environmental
protection.

The Company has devised a policy on Board diversity (the “Policy”)         本公司已制定董事會成員多元化政策（「政
to ensure adequate diversity in its Board, with effect from 1 January      策 」）以 確 保 董 事 會 充 分 多 元 化 ， 自 2019

Vision                                                                     願景

The Company believes diversity is important to enhance the Board’s         本公司深信，多元化政策能夠鼓勵不同觀
effectiveness by encouraging a diversity of perspectives and to            點百花齊放，對提升董事會效能及維持高
maintain high standards of corporate governance. The Company will          水平企業管治至關重要。本公司將繼續因
continue to monitor and develop new objectives for implementing            應本公司和市場不時的具體需求，在其認
and achieving improved diversity on the Board as and when it               為有需要時監察及訂立新目標，藉以提升
considers appropriate with regard to the specific needs of the             董事會的多元性。
Company and the market from time to time.


Policy Statement                                                           政策聲明

The Company recognises and embraces the benefits of having a               本公司認可到並接納董事會多元化的成
diversified Board and sees increasing diversity at the Board level as an   效，並將董事會層面的多元化視為保持本
essential element in maintaining the Company’s competitive                 公司競爭優勢的一個重要因素。
advantage.

knowledge and the like. The ultimate decision will be based on             終將按入選候選人可為本公司及董事會帶
overall contribution that the selected candidates will bring to the        來的整體貢獻而定。
Board and the Company.

     HKFRS 16 Leases                                                           香港財務報告準則第16號租賃
     HKFRS 16 introduces a comprehensive model for the                         香 港 財 務 報 告 準 則 第 16 號 為 識 別 出
     identification of lease arrangements and accounting treatments            租人及承租人的租賃安排及會計處
     for both lessors and lessees. HKFRS 16 will supersede HKAS 17             理引入一個綜合模式。當香港財務
     Leases and the related interpretations when it becomes                    報 告 準 則 第 16 號 生 效 時 ， 將 取 代 香
     effective.                                                                港會計準則第17號租賃及相關詮釋。


     HKFRS 16 distinguishes lease and service contracts on the basis           香 港 財 務 報 告 準 則 第 16 號 根 據 所 識
     of whether an identified asset is controlled by a customer. In            別資產是否由客戶控制來區分租賃
     addition, HKFRS 16 requires sales and leaseback transactions to           及服務合約。此外，香港財務報告
     be determined based on the requirements of HKFRS 15 as to                 準 則 第 16 號 規 定 售 後 租 回 交 易 根 據
     whether the transfer of the relevant asset should be accounted            香 港 財 務 報 告 準 則 第 15 號 有 關 轉 讓
     as a sale. HKFRS 16 also includes requirements relating to                相關資產是否應作為銷售入賬的規

3. SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                         3.   主要會計政策

      The consolidated financial statements have been prepared in               綜合財務報表乃根據香港會計師公
      accordance with HKFRSs issued by the HKICPA. In addition, the             會頒布之香港財務報告準則編製。
      consolidated financial statements include applicable disclosures          此外，綜合財務報表包括聯交所證
      required by the Rules Governing the Listing of Securities on the          券 上 市 規 則（「 上 市 規 則 」）及 香 港 公
      Stock Exchange (the “Listing Rules”) and the Hong Kong                    司條例規定之適用披露資料。
      Companies Ordinance.


      The consolidated financial statements have been prepared on               綜合財務報表按歷史成本法編製，
      the historical cost basis except for certain financial instruments        惟於每個報告期末按公允值計量之
      that are measured at fair values at the end of each reporting             若干金融工具除外，會計政策闡述
      period, as explained in the accounting policies set out below.            於下文。


      Historical cost is generally based on the fair value of the               歷史成本一般按照交換貨物所付代
      consideration given in exchange for goods.                                價之公允值。


      Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an asset or        公允值為於計量日期在市場參與者
      paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction between            之間進行有序交易就出售資產所收
      market participants at the measurement date, regardless of                取或轉讓負債所支付的價格，不論
      whether that price is directly observable or estimated using              該價格是直接可觀察或使用另一種
      another valuation technique. In estimating the fair value of an           估值方式估計得出。在估計一項資
      asset or a liability, the Group takes into account the                    產或負債之公允值時，本集團考慮
      characteristic of the asset or liability if market participants           市場參與者於計量日期為該資產或
      would take those characteristics into account when pricing the            負債定價時會考慮之特質。就於該
      asset or liability at the measurement date. Fair value for                等綜合財務報表中計量及╱或披露
      measurement and/or disclosure purposes in these consolidated              之公允值乃按此基準釐定，惟於香
      financial statements is determined on such a basis, except for            港財務報告準則第2號以股份為基礎
      share-based payment transactions that are within the scope of             付款範疇下的以股份為基礎付款之
      HKFRS 2 Share-based Payment, leasing transactions that are                交 易 、 香 港 會 計 準 則 第 17 號 租 賃 範
      within the scope of HKAS 17 Leases, and measurements that                 疇下的租賃交易及一些類似公允值
      have some similarities to fair value but are not fair value, such         但非公允值的計量（例如香港會計準
      as net realisable value in HKAS 2 Inventories, or value in use in         則第2號存貨之可變現淨值或香港會
      HKAS 36 Impairment of Assets.                                             計準則第36號資產減值的使用價值）
                                                                                則除外。

     (CONTINUED)

     In addition, for financial reporting purpose, fair value                此外，就財務申報而言，公允值計
     measurements are categorised into Level 1, 2 or 3 based on the          量根據公允值計量輸入數據之可觀
     degree to which the inputs to the fair value measurements are           察程度及輸入數據對公允值整體計

     •    Level 1 inputs are quoted prices (unadjusted) in active            •   第1級輸入數據為實體於計量
          markets for identical assets or liabilities that the entity            日期在活躍市場就相同資產或
          can access at the measurement date;                                    負債取得之報價（未經調整）；


     •    Level 2 inputs are inputs, other than quoted prices                •   第2級輸入數據為就資產或負
          included within Level 1, that are observable for the asset             債直接或間接可觀察之輸入數
          or liability, either directly or indirectly; and                       據，包括在第一級內之報價除
                                                                                 外；及


     •    Level 3 inputs are unobservable inputs for the asset or            •   第3級輸入數據為資產或負債
          liability.                                                             的不可觀察輸入數據。

3. SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                         3.   主要會計政策（續）
      (CONTINUED)

      The principal accounting policies are set out below.                      主要會計政策載列如下：


      Basis of consolidation                                                    綜合賬目基準

      The consolidated financial statements incorporate the financial           本綜合財務報表包含本公司以及由
      statements of the Company and entities controlled by the                  本公司及其附屬公司控制的實體之
      Company and its subsidiaries. Control is achieved where the               財務報表。取得控制權是指本公司：
      Company:


      •     has power over the investee;                                        •   對被投資方行使權力；


      •     is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from its             •   因參與被投資方之業務而獲得
            involvement with the investee; and                                      或有權獲得可變回報；及


      •     has the ability to use its power to affect its returns.             •   有能力行使其權力而影響其回
                                                                                    報。


      The Group reassesses whether or not it controls an investee if            倘事實及情況顯示上述三項控制因
      facts and circumstances indicate that there are changes to one            素其中一項或多項改變，則本集團

                                                                                方。


      Consolidation of a subsidiary begins when the Group obtains               當本集團取得附屬公司之控制權，
      control over the subsidiary and ceases when the Group loses               便將該附屬公司綜合入賬；當本
      control of the subsidiary. Specifically, income and expenses of a         集團失去附屬公司之控制權，便停
      subsidiary acquired or disposed of during the year are included           止將該附屬公司綜合入賬。具體而
      in the consolidated statement of profit or loss and other                 言，年內收購或出售之附屬公司之
      comprehensive income from the date the Group gains control                收入及支出會於本集團取得控制權
      until the date when the Group ceases to control the subsidiary.           當日起計入綜合損益及其他全面收
                                                                                益表，直至本集團對該附屬公司之
                                                                                控制權終止當日為止。


      Profit or loss and each component of other comprehensive                  損益及其他全面收益的各個項目乃
      income are attributed to the owners of the Company and to                 歸屬於本公司擁有人及非控股權
      the non-controlling interests. Total comprehensive income of              益。即使會導致非控股權益出現虧
      subsidiaries is attributed to the owners of the Company and to            絀結餘，附屬公司之總全面收益仍
      the non-controlling interests even if this results in the non-            會歸屬於本公司擁有人及非控股權
      controlling interests having a deficit balance.                           益。

     Investment in a joint venture                                           合營企業權益

     A joint venture is a joint arrangement whereby the parties that         合營企業是一種共同安排，據此共
     have joint control of the arrangement have rights to the net            同控制該安排的各方擁有該安排的
     assets of the joint arrangement. Joint control is the                   淨資產。共同控制為一項合約協定
     contractually agreed sharing of control of an arrangement,              共享控制之安排，僅會於決定相關
     which exists only when decisions about the relevant activities          事項須共享控制的各方一致同意時
     require unanimous consent of the parties sharing control.               方會出現。


     The results and assets and liabilities of joint venture are             合營企業之業績及資產與負債乃按
     incorporated in these consolidated financial statements using           權益會計法列入綜合財務報表內。

     prepared using uniform accounting policies as those of the              交易及事件所採用的劃一會計政策
     Group for like transactions and events in similar circumstances.        而編製。根據權益法，合營企業權
     Under the equity method, an investment in a joint venture is            益於綜合財務狀況表初始按成本確
     initially recognised in the consolidated statement of financial         認，及其後調整以確認本集團應佔
     position at cost and adjusted thereafter to recognise the               該合營企業之損益及其他全面收
     Group’s share of the profit or loss and other comprehensive             益。合營企業淨資產於損益及其他
     income of the joint venture. Changes in net assets of the joint         全面收益表以外之變動毋須入賬，
     venture other than profit or loss and other comprehensive               除非該變動是由於本集團擁有權權
     income are not accounted for unless such changes resulted in            益變動所致。當本集團應佔合營企
     changes in ownership interest held by the Group. When the               業之虧損超出其於該合營企業之權
     Group’s share of losses of a joint venture exceeds the Group’s          益（ 即 包 括 任 何 實 質 上 構 成 本 集 團

     investment in the joint venture), the Group discontinues                一步虧損。僅會於本集團已產生法
     recognising its share of further losses. Additional losses are          定或推定責任或替該合營企業支付

     or constructive obligations or made payments on behalf of the

      amount. Any impairment loss recognised forms part of the                    較高者減出售成本）加以比較。任何

           an alternative use to the Group and the Group has an                        團有其他用途的資產及本集團
           enforceable right to payment for performance completed                      對迄今完成的履約之付款有可
           to date.                                                                    執行之權利。

     Revenue recognition (prior to 1 January 2018)                            收入確認（在2018年1月1日之前）

     been met for each of the Group’s activities, as described below.         具體標準時確認，載述如下。

     probable that the economic benefits will flow to the Group and           將可能流入本集團而其收入金額能
     the amount of income can be measured reliably. Interest                  可靠地計量時確認。金融資產之利

      Leases                                                                     租賃

      Leases are classified as finance leases whenever the terms of              凡租賃之條款規定擁有權所附帶之
      the lease transfer substantially all the risks and rewards of              一切風險及回報實質上轉移至承租
      ownership to the lessee. All other leases are classified as                者，該租賃即歸類為融資租賃。其
      operating leases.                                                          他租賃全部列作經營租賃。


      The Group as lessor                                                        集團作為出租人
      Rental income from operating leases is recognised on a                     經營租賃產生之租金收入按直線法
      straight-line basis over the term of relevant lease. Initial direct        於相關租賃期內在損益確認。磋商
      cost incurred in negotiating and arranging an operating lease              及安排一項經營租賃涉及的初始直
      are added to the carrying amount of the leased asset and                   接成本乃加至租賃資產之賬面值及
      recognised on a straight-line basis over the lease term.                   以直線法於租賃期內確認。


      The Group as lessee                                                        集團作為承租人
      Operating lease payments, including the cost of acquiring land             經營租賃款項（包括購置持有作經營
      held under operating leases, are recognised as an expense on a             租賃之土地成本）乃按租賃年期以直
      straight-line basis over the lease term.                                   線法確認為費用。


      Leasehold land and buildings                                               租賃土地及樓宇
      When the Group makes payments for a property interest which                當本集團向一項包括租賃土地及樓
      includes both leasehold land and building elements, the Group              宇部分之物業權益付款時，本集
      assesses the classification of each element as a finance or an             團以評估與各部分擁有權有關的一
      operating lease separately based on the assessment as to                   切風險及回報是否已實質上轉移至
      whether substantially all the risks and rewards incidental to              本集團為基礎，評估如何將各部分
      ownership of each element have been transferred to the                     分類為融資或經營租賃，除非肯定
      Group, unless it is clear that both elements are operating                 兩部分均為經營租賃，則於該情況
      leases, in which case the entire property is accounted as an               下，整項租賃乃分類為經營租賃。
      operating lease. Specifically, the entire consideration (including         尤其，全數代價（包括任何一次性預
      any lump-sum upfront payments) are allocated between the                   付款項）乃按初始確認時土地部分及
      leasehold land and the building elements in proportion to the              樓宇部分的租賃權益之相關公允值
      relative fair values of the leasehold interests in the land element        比例，於租賃土地及樓宇部分之間
      and building element at initial recognition.                               進行分配。


      To the extent the allocation of the relevant payments can be               倘有關款項能夠可靠分配時，則入
      made reliably, interest in leasehold land that is accounted for as         賬列作經營租賃的租賃土地權益乃
      an operating lease is presented as “prepaid lease payments” in             於綜合財務狀況表列作「預付租賃款
      the consolidated statement of financial position and is                    項」，並以直線法於租賃期內攤銷。
      amortised over the lease term on a straight-line basis.

     Foreign currencies                                                      外幣

     In preparing the financial statements of each individual group          於編製各個別集團實體之財務報表
     entity, transactions in currencies other than the entity’s              時，以該實體功能貨幣以外之貨幣
     functional currency (foreign currencies) are recognised at the          （ 外 幣 ）進 行 之 交 易 均 按 交 易 日 期 之
     rates of exchanges prevailing on the dates of the transactions.         適用換算滙率確認。於報告期末，
     At the end of the reporting period, monetary items                      以外幣定值之貨幣項目均按結算日
     denominated in foreign currencies are retranslated at the rates         之適用滙率重新換算。非貨幣項目
     prevailing at that date. Non-monetary items that are measured           按外幣過往成本計量而毋須重新換
     in terms of historical cost in a foreign currency are not               算。
     retranslated.


     Exchange differences arising on the settlement of and on the            結算及重新換算貨幣項目所產生之
     retranslation of monetary items are recognised in profit or loss        匯兌差額於其產生期間於損益內確
     in the period in which they arise, except for exchange                  認，惟源自其結算並無計劃及不大
     differences on monetary items receivable from or payable to a           可能出現的應收或應付境外業務之
     foreign operation for which settlement is neither planned nor           貨幣項目的匯兌差額（因此構成境外
     likely to occur (therefore forming part of the net investment in        業務淨投資之一部分）除外，該差額
     the foreign operation), which are recognised initially in other         初始於其他全面收益表確認及當出
     comprehensive income and reclassified from equity to profit or          售或部分出售本集團之權益時重新
     loss on disposal or partial disposal of the Group’s interests.          由權益分類至損益。


     For the purposes of presenting the consolidated financial               就呈列綜合財務報表而言，本集團
     statements, the assets and liabilities of the Group’s foreign           海外經營業務之資產及負債乃採用
     operations are translated into the presentation currency of the         報告期末當日之滙率換算為本集團
     Group (i.e. HK$) using exchange rates prevailing at the end of          之 列 賬 貨 幣（ 即 港 元 ）， 而 收 入 及 支
     each reporting period. income and expenses items are                    出項目乃按該期間之平均滙率進行
     translated at the average exchange rates for the period, unless         換算，除非滙率於該期間內出現大
     exchange rates fluctuate significantly during the period, in            幅波動則作別論，於此情況下，則
     which case, the exchange rates prevailing at the dates of               採用於交易當日之匯率。所產生之
     transactions are used. Exchange differences arising, if any, are        匯 兌 差 額（ 如 有 ）乃 於 其 他 全 面 收 益
     recognised in other comprehensive income and accumulated in             確認及於滙兌儲備項下累計權益。
     equity under the heading of translation reserve.

      recognised as an expense when the employees have rendered                付款於僱員提供服務使其可享有供
      services entitling them to the contributions.                            款時確認為費用。

      and when employees rendered services. All short-term                     認。所有短期僱員福利確認為費

      HKFRS requires or permits the inclusion of the benefit in the            定或准許將福利概括入一項資產的
      cost of an asset.                                                        成本中。

     Taxation                                                                  稅項

     Income tax expense represents the sum of the tax currently                利得稅支出指現時應付稅項及遞延
     payable and deferred tax.                                                 稅項的總和。


     The tax currently payable is based on taxable profit for the year.        現時應付稅項乃按年度應課稅盈利
     Taxable profit differs from ’profit before tax’ as reported in the        計算。應課稅盈利與綜合損益及其
     consolidated statement of profit or loss and other comprehensive          他全面收益表中所報除稅前盈利不
     income because of items of income or expense that are taxable             同乃源於其他年度應課稅或可扣稅
     or deductible in other years and items that are never taxable or          的收入或開支項目及從未課稅及扣
     deductible. The Group’s current tax is calculated using tax rates         稅的項目。本集團之本期稅項乃按
     that have been enacted or substantively enacted by the end of             報告期末已實行或大致上已實行之
     the reporting period.                                                     稅率計算。


     Deferred tax is recognised on temporary differences between               遞延稅項乃根據綜合財務報表之資
     the carrying amounts of assets and liabilities in the consolidated        產及負債賬面值與計算應課稅盈
     financial statements and the corresponding tax base used in               利所採用相應稅基的暫時差額而確
     the computation of taxable profit. Deferred tax liabilities are           認。遞延稅項負債一般就所有應課
     generally recognised for all taxable temporary differences.               稅暫時差額予以確認。遞延稅項資
     Deferred tax assets are generally recognised for all deductible           產一般就所有可扣稅暫時差額確
     temporary differences to the extent that it is probable that              認，惟僅於可能有應課稅盈利將可
     taxable profits will be available against which those deductible          以使用該可扣稅暫時差額時確認。
     temporary differences can be utilised. Such deferred assets and           倘暫時差額乃由初始確認一項不影
     liabilities are not recognised if the temporary difference arises         響應課稅盈利或會計盈利的交易的
     from the initial recognition of assets and liabilities in a               其他資產及負債所引起，則有關遞
     transaction that affects neither the taxable profit nor the               延資產及負債不予確認。
     accounting profit.


     Deferred tax liabilities are recognised for taxable temporary             遞延稅項負債一般就與附屬公司之
     differences associated with investments in subsidiaries and               投資及合營企業權益而引致之應課
     interest in joint venture, except where the Group is able to              稅暫時差額而確認，惟若本集團可
     control the reversal of the temporary difference and it is                控制暫時差額之撥回及暫時差額有
     probable that the temporary difference will not reverse in the            可能不會於可見將來撥回之情況則
     foreseeable future. Deferred tax assets arising from deductible           除外。與該等投資及權益相關的可
     temporary differences associated with such investments and                扣稅暫時差額所產生的遞延稅項資
     interest are only recognised to the extent that it is probable            產，僅在可能有足夠應課稅盈利可
     that there will be sufficient taxable profits against which to            以使用暫時差額之效益且預計於可
     utilise the benefits of the temporary differences and they are            見將來可以撥回時確認。
     expected to reverse in the foreseeable future.

      Taxation (Continued)                                                        稅項
                                                                                   （續）

      The carrying amount of deferred tax assets is reviewed at the               遞延稅項資產之賬面值於各報告期
      end of the reporting period and reduced to the extent that it is            末作檢討，並在沒可能會有足夠應
      no longer probable that sufficient taxable profits will be                  課稅盈利以收回全部或部分資產時
      available to allow all or part of the asset to be recovered.                加以遞減。


      Deferred tax assets and liabilities are measured at the tax rates           遞延稅項資產及負債按預期負債清
      that are expected to apply in the period in which the liability is          償或資產變現期間內應用之稅率，
      settled or the asset is realised, based on tax rate (and tax laws)          以報告期末已生效或實際上已生效
      that have been enacted or substantively enacted by the end of               之稅率（及稅法）為基準計量。
      the reporting period.


      The measurement of deferred tax liabilities and assets reflects             遞延稅項負債及資產之計量反映本
      the tax consequences that would follow from the manner in                   集團於報告期末所預期對收回或償
      which the Group expects, at the end of the reporting period, to             還其資產及負債之賬面值之方式所
      recover or settle the carrying amount of its assets and liabilities.        產生的稅務後果。


      Deferred tax assets and liabilities are offset when there is a              當有法定行使權以本期稅項資產抵
      legally enforceable right to set off current tax assets against             銷本期稅項負債，及當該些稅項為
      current tax liabilities and when they relate to income taxes                同一稅務機關所徵收之所得稅，且
      levied by the same taxation authority and the Group intends to              本集團有意將其本期稅項資產及負
      settle its current tax assets and liabilities on a net basis.               債以淨額結算，遞延稅項資產及負
                                                                                  債會被抵銷。


      Current and deferred tax are recognised in profit or loss, except           本期及遞延稅項於損益確認，惟倘
      when they relate to items that are recognised in other                      稅項涉及於其他全面收益或直接於
      comprehensive income or directly in equity, in which case, the              權益確認之項目，在此情況下，本
      current and deferred tax are also recognised in other                       期及遞延稅項亦會分別於其他全面
      comprehensive income or directly in equity respectively.                    收益或直接於權益內確認。

     Property, plant and equipment                                             物業、廠房及設備

     impairment losses, if any.

     impairment loss. Costs include professional fees and, for                 賬。成本包括專業費用，及對合資

     Depreciation of these assets, on the same basis as other                  等資產的折舊按其他物業資產之相

     intended use.                                                             開始計提折舊。


     Depreciation is recognised so as to write off the cost of assets,         物 業 、 廠 房 及 設 備（ 在 建 工 程 除 外 ）
     other than construction in progress, less their residual values           之折舊乃按其估計可使用年期，於

     The estimated useful lives, residual values and depreciation              撇銷其成本。估計可使用年期、剩
     method are reviewed at the end of each reporting period, with             餘價值及折舊方法於各報告期末予
     the effect of any changes in estimate accounted for on a                  以檢討，任何估計變動的影響按前
     prospective basis.                                                        瞻基準入賬。

      determine whether there is any indication that those assets             是否有任何跡象顯示該等資產出現

      order to determine the extent of the impairment loss, if any.           釐定減值虧損的幅度（如有）。

     Impairment on tangible assets (Continued)                                有形資產減值
                                                                                   （續）

     The recoverable amount of tangible assets are estimated                  有形資產的可收回金額會個別估
     individually, when it is not possible to estimate the recoverable        計，倘不能個別估計資產的可收回
     amount of an asset individually, the Group estimates the                 金額，則本集團會估計其資產所屬
     recoverable amount of the cash-generating unit to which the              現金產生單位的可收回金額。當可
     asset belongs. Where a reasonable and consistent basis of                識別分配基準屬合理及貫徹一致
     allocation can be identified, corporate assets are also allocated        時，企業資產亦會被分配到個別的
     to individual cash-generating units, or otherwise they are               現金產生單位，否則或會被分配到
     allocated to the smallest group of cash-generating units for             可識別分配基準屬合理及貫徹一致
     which a reasonable and consistent allocation basis can be                的最小的現金產生單位中。
     identified.


     Recoverable amount is the higher of fair value less costs of             可收回金額為公允值減出售成本或
     disposal and value in use. In assessing value in use, the                使用價值之較高者。評估使用價值
     estimated future cash flows are discounted to their present              時，估計未來現金流量乃使用稅前
     value using a pre-tax discount rate that reflects current market         貼現率折現至其貼現值，該貼現率
     assessments of the time value of money and the risks specific            反映目前市場對資金時間值之評估
     to the asset (or a cash-generating unit) for which the estimates         以及估計未來現金流量未經調整之
     of future cash flows have not been adjusted.                             資產（或現金產生單位）的獨立風險。


     If the recoverable amount of an asset (or a cash-generating              倘 某 項 資 產（ 或 現 金 產 生 單 位 ）之 可
     unit) is estimated to be less than its carrying amount, the              收回金額估計低於其賬面值，則該
     carrying amount of the asset (or a cash-generating unit) is              項 資 產（ 或 現 金 產 生 單 位 ）之 賬 面 值
     reduced to its recoverable amount. An impairment loss is                 須削減至其可收回金額。減值虧損
     recognised immediately in profit or loss.                                即時在損益中確認。


     Where an impairment loss subsequently reverses, the carrying             倘日後撥回減值虧損，有關資產（或
     amount of the asset (or cash-generating unit) is increased to            現金產生單位）之賬面值須增加至其
     the revised estimate of its recoverable amount, but so that the          經重新估計之可收回金額，惟增加
     increased carrying amount does not exceed the carrying                   之賬面值不得超出倘有關資產（或現
     amount that would have been determined had no impairment                 金產生單位）並無於過往年度確認任
     loss been recognised for the asset (or a cash-generating unit) in        何減值虧損所應釐定之賬面值。撥
     prior years. A reversal of an impairment loss is recognised              回之減值虧損即時在損益中確認。
     immediately in profit or loss.

      Inventories                                                                  存貨

      Inventories are stated at the lower of cost and net realisable               存貨按成本或可變現淨值兩者中之
      value. Costs of inventories are determined on a first-in, first-out          較低者列賬。存貨成本按先入先出
      basis. Net realisable value represents the estimated selling price           基準釐定。可變現淨值指存貨估計
      for inventories less all estimated costs of completion and costs             售價減估計完成所需之全部成本以
      necessary to make the sales.                                                 及銷售所需之成本。


      Financial instruments                                                        金融工具

      Financial assets and financial liabilities are recognised when a             金融資產及金融負債於集團實體成
      group entity becomes a party to the contractual provisions of                為工具訂約條文之訂約方時確認。
      the instruments.


      Financial assets and financial liabilities are initially measured at         金融資產及金融負債乃按公平值作
      fair value except for trade receivables arising from contracts               初始計量，惟來自客戶合約而產生
      with customers which are initially measured in accordance with               的應收貿易賬款則自2018年1月1日
      HKFRS 15 since 1 January 2018. Transaction costs that are                    起 根 據 香 港 財 務 報 告 準 則 第 15 號 作
      directly attributable to the acquisition or issue of financial assets        初始計量。因收購或發行金融資產
      and financial liabilities (other than financial assets and financial         及金融負債（按公允值計入損益之金
      liabilities at fair value through profit or loss) are added to or            融資產及金融負債除外）而直接應佔
      deducted from the fair value of the financial assets or financial            之交易成本於初始確認時，按適用
      liabilities, as appropriate, on initial recognition. Transaction             情況加入或扣自該項金融資產或金
      costs directly attributable to the acquisition of financial assets           融負債之公允值。因收購按公允值
      or financial liabilities at fair value through profit or loss are            計入損益之金融資產及金融負債而
      recognised immediately in profit or loss.                                    直接應佔之交易成本即時於損益確
                                                                                   認。

           Despite the aforegoing, the Group assumes that the                           儘管如此，若債務工具於報告
           credit risk on a debt instrument has not increased                           日期被釐定為低信貸風險，本
           significantly since initial recognition if the debt instrument               集團則假設該債務工具之信
           is determined to have low credit risk at the reporting                       貸風險自初始確認以來並無顯
           date. A debt instrument is determined to have low credit                     著增加。債務工具被釐定為低
           risk if i) it has a low risk of default; ii) the borrower has a              信 貸 風 險 ， 倘 i ）其 違 約 風 險 屬
           strong capacity to meet its contractual cash flow                            低；ii）借款人具強勁能力於近
           obligations in the near term; and iii) adverse changes in                    期履行其合約現金流量責任；
           economic and business conditions in the longer term                          及iii）長遠的經濟及業務狀況不
           may, but will not necessarily, reduce the ability of the                     利逆轉可能，但不一定，減低
           borrower to fulfil its contractual cash flow obligations.                    借款人履行其合約現金流量責
           The Group considers a debt instrument to have low credit                     任的能力。當債務工具於內部
           risk when it has an internal or external credit rating of                    或外部信貸評級為按全球理解
           ‘investment grade’ as per globally understood definitions.                   定義的「投資級別」，本集團認
                                                                                        為該債務工具屬低風險。


           The Group regularly monitors the effectiveness of the                        本集團定期監控用作識別是否
           criteria used to identify whether there has been a                           出現信貸風險顯著增加的標準
           significant increase in credit risk and revises them as                      之有效性及當適用時修訂標準
           appropriate to ensure that the criteria are capable of                       以確保該標準乃於金額逾期前
           identifying significant increase in credit risk before the                   可識別信貸風險的顯著增加。
           amount becomes past due.

               For internal credit risk management, the Group considers                  就內部信貸風險管理，當內部

               Group, in full (without taking into account any collaterals               本 集 團 持 有 任 何 抵 押 品 ）， 本
               held by the Group).                                                       集團認為發生違約事件。

     (iii)   Credit-impaired financial assets                                    (iii) 信貸減值金融資產
             A financial asset is credit-impaired when one or more                     當一項及多項違約事件發生令
             events of default that have a detrimental impact on the                   該金融資產估計未來現金流量
             estimated future cash flows of that financial asset have                  具不利影響，金融資產則信貸
             occurred. Evidence that a financial asset is credit-impaired              減值。金融資產減值的證據包
             includes observable data about the following events:                      括以下事件之可觀察數據：


             (a)   significant financial difficulty of the issuer or the               (a)   發行人或借款人存在嚴重
                   borrower;                                                                 財政困難；


             (b)   a breach of contract, such as a default or past due                 (b)   違約，即如違約或過期事
                   event;                                                                    件；


             (c)   the lender(s) of the borrower, for economic or                      (c)   借款人之貸款人，就有關
                   contractual reasons relating to the borrower’s                            借款人財政困難之經濟或
                   financial difficulty, having granted to the borrower                      合約原因，給予借款人一
                   a concession(s) that the lender(s) would not                              般貸款人不會考慮的讓
                   otherwise consider;                                                       步；

                The Group writes off a financial asset when there is                        當有資料顯示對手方存在嚴重

                financial difficulty and there is no realistic prospect of                  款項，例如，當對手方已進行
                recovery, for example, when the counterparty has been                       清盤或已進入破產程序，或如
                placed under liquidation or has entered into bankruptcy                     為應收貿易賬款，當金額乃逾
                proceedings, or in the case of trade receivables, when the                  期超過兩年，以較早者為準，
                amounts are over two years past due, whichever occurs                       本集團撇銷金融資產。金融資
                sooner. Financial assets written off may still be subject to                產的撇銷可能仍須根據本集團
                enforcement activities under the Group’s recovery                           回收程序的執行方式，當適用
                procedures, taking into account legal advice where                          時考慮法律建議。撇銷構成剔
                appropriate. A write-off constitutes a derecognition                        除確認事項。任何其後回撥於
                event. Any subsequent recoveries are recognised in profit                   損益確認。
                or loss.

     (v)   Measurement and recognition of ECL                                  (v)   預期信貸虧損的計量及確認
           The measurement of ECL is a function of the probability                   預期信貸虧損計量具違約可能
           of default, loss given default (i.e. the magnitude of the                 性 ， 默 認 虧 損（ 即 違 約 的 損 失
           loss if there is a default) and the exposure at default. The              幅 度 ）及 違 約 曝 光 的 功 能 。 違
           assessment of the probability of default and loss given                   約可能性及默認虧損的評估乃
           default is based on historical data adjusted by forward-                  基於過往數據經前瞻性資料作
           looking information. Estimation of ECL reflects an                        調整。預期信貸虧損的估計反
           unbiased and probability-weighted amount that is                          映不偏倚及概率加權金額乃按
           determined with the respective risks of default occurring                 發生違約相關風險的比重來釐
           as the weights.                                                           定。


           Generally, the ECL is the difference between all                          一般而言，預期信貸虧損為根
           contractual cash flows that are due to the Group in                       據合約所有應欠本集團合約現
           accordance with the contract and the cash flows that the                  金流量與本集團於初始確認時
           Group expects to receive, discounted at the effective                     預期收到，按實際利率折現的
           interest rate determined at initial recognition.                          現金流量的差額。

     period. The resulting gain or loss is recognised in profit or loss.        收益或虧損於損益內確認。

      receivables. The provision rates are based on internal credit            式計算預期信貸虧損。撥備率根據

      information that is reasonable and supportable available                 性資料，乃合理及毋須不必要的成
      without undue costs or effort. At every reporting date, the              本或費力而可得之理據。於每一報
      historical observed default rates are reassessed and changes in          告日期，會再次評估過往觀測的違
      the forward-looking information are considered. In addition,             約率並考慮前贍性資料的變化。此
      trade receivables with significant balances and credit impaired          外，具重大結餘及信貸減值的應收

32. RETIREMENT BENEFITS PLANS                                         32. 退休福利計劃

     Defined contribution scheme                                        界定供款計劃

     The Group operates a MPF Scheme for all qualifying employees       本集團在香港為所有合資格僱員設
     in Hong Kong. The assets of the MPF scheme are held                立一項強積金計劃。強積金計劃
     separately from those of the Group, in funds under the control     的資產與本集團的資產分開持有，
     of trustees. The Group contributes monthly the lower of            以及撥入信託人管理的基金。本集
     HK$1,500 or 5% of the relevant monthly payroll costs to the        團 按 1,500 港 元 或 有 關 月 薪 成 本 的
     MPF Scheme, which contribution is matched by employees.            5%，以較低者為準，向強積金計劃
                                                                        供款，與僱員之供款一致。


     The only obligation of the Group with respect of MPF Scheme is     本集團對強積金計劃的唯一責任就
     to make the required contribution payable on the future years.     是在未來年度作出所需應付供款。


     The employees employed in the PRC subsidiaries are members         中國附屬公司聘用的僱員均參與中
     of the state-managed retirement benefit scheme operated by         國政府營辦的國家管理退休福利
     the PRC government. The PRC subsidiaries are required to           計劃。中國附屬公司須按僱員薪金
     contribute a certain percentage of their payroll to the            的若干百分比，向退休福利計劃供
     retirement benefit scheme to fund the benefits. The only           款，作為福利之用。本集團對退休
     obligation of the Group with respect to the retirement benefit     福利計劃的唯一責任就是作出指定
     scheme is to make the specified contributions.                     供款。


     The total expense recognised in profit or loss of                  於 損 益 中 確 認 之 總 支 出 82,120,000
     HK$82,120,000 (2017: HK$58,545,000) represents                     港元（2017年：58,545,000港元）為
     contributions payable to these plans by the Group at rates         本集團按計劃規定之指定比率就該

     contributions of HK$58,340 (2017: HK$21,000) due in respect        31 日 ， 就 截 至 2018 年 12 月 31 日 止

34. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                               34. 資本風險管理

      The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in the          本集團管理其資本以確保本集團的
      Group will be able to continue as a going concern while               實體可持續經營業務，並透過最佳
      maximising the return to stakeholders through the optimisation        債務及權益比例為權益持有人提供
      of the debt and equity balance. The Group’s overall strategy          最高回報。本集團的整體政策維持
      remains unchanged from prior year.                                    與去年一致。


      The capital structure of the Group consists of debts, which           本集團的資本結構包括債務，當中
      include the bank borrowings, and equity attributable to equity        包括銀行借貸以及本公司權益持有
      holders of the Company, comprising issued share capital and           人應佔權益（包括已發行股本及多項
      various reserves.                                                     儲備）。


      The directors of the Company review the capital structure on a        本公司董事不時檢討資本結構。作
      regular basis. As part of this review, the directors consider the     為檢討的一部分，董事考慮資本成
      cost of capital and the risks associated with each class of           本以及與各類資本有關的風險。根
      capital. Based on recommendations of the directors, the Group         據董事的建議，本集團將通過派付
      will balance its overall capital structure through the payment of     股息、發行新股及購回股份以及發
      dividends, new share issues and share buy-backs as well as the        行新債或贖回現債平衡其整體資本
      issue of new debt or the redemption of existing debt.                 結構。
